What is Internal Knowledge Search for Enterprise?

For Enterprise users, Internal Knowledge Search integrates your organization’s internal files with Perplexity’s search capabilities, enhancing collaboration and productivity within your team.

Lean more about Perplexity Enterprise.

Why use Internal Knowledge Search?

  • Comprehensive research: Combine insights from the web with proprietary internal information for richer, more specific answers.
  • Enhanced collaboration: Empower your team to ask questions and get answers based on both web sources and your organization’s internal documents, enabling informed decision-making without the need to manually access or share files.
  • Increased productivity: Save time by searching across all relevant organization documents instantly.

How does Internal Knowledge Search work for Enterprise users?

Enterprise users can leverage Internal Knowledge Search in two main ways:

  1. Using your organization’s file repository

  • Administrators upload files: Your organization’s administrators upload files to a central repository, which Perplexity can use as sources when answering questions from anyone on your team.

  • How to use files as sources: In Perplexity’s search bar, look for Web + Org Files. When both are selected, Perplexity will use your organization’s files and sources from the web to synthesize answers.

  • Deselect Web if you want Perplexity to only use your files as sources.

  • Deselect Org Files if you want Perplexity to only use web sources.

  • Deselect both if you want Perplexity to respond without external sources.

  • Receiving comprehensive answers: Perplexity determines which internal files are relevant and uses the most pertinent sections within those files to form its responses.

  • File citations: Insights derived from your internal files will include in-line citations, so you know exactly where the information is coming from. You can download and open files directly from the citation references to explore the content further.

  1. Creating and using Perplexity projects

  • Creating a project: Enterprise users can create Perplexity Projects to organize their sessions and collaborate with team members. With Projects, you can create custom versions of Perplexity tailored to specific projects or topics.

  • Uploading files to the project: Files uploaded to a project will be used as sources for search sessions within that Project, giving you customized search results based on your internal documents.

  • Files uploaded to an individual user’s projects are not included in the overall organization’s file repository.

  • Inviting collaborators: For security purposes, only users within your organization can be invited to join a project. Colleagues you invite to collaborate in a project will be able to start new sessions or continue existing ones, utilizing the files and resources uploaded to that Project.

  • Personalized search experience: Projects offer additional customization options:

  • Upload specific internal knowledge sets: Choose which files or datasets are used as sources in your projects.

  • Select from advanced AI models: Choose which AI model will craft responses to your questions, including GPT-5 and Claude 4.5 Sonnet.

  • Set custom instructions: Provide specific guidelines or a persona for how Perplexity should construct its answers.

Learn more about Perplexity projects.

File Upload vs. Internal Knowledge Search

  • File upload:

  • You can attach up to five files per thread directly in the search bar.

  • Files are used temporarily within that session and are deleted after seven days.

  • Ideal for one-time queries with specific files as input.

  • Internal Knowledge Search for Enterprise:

  • Organization files: Enterprise administrators can upload up to 500 files to the organization’s repository, accessible to all users within your organization.

  • Perplexity projects: Individual Enterprise users can upload their own files to a project for use in search sessions within that Project.

  • Files remain accessible to all users within your organization until manually removed or updated by an administrator.

  • Enables comprehensive search across your organization’s entire internal knowledge base.

Learn more about File Upload.

How do I select sources for my search?

  • Click “Choose sources” in your search bar.

  • Choose from the following options:

  • Web: Perplexity will only use sources from the internet to generate answers

  • Org Files: Perplexity will only information contained in your organization’s file repository to generate answers

  • Web + Org Files: Perplexity will use both web sources and your organization’s file repository to generate answers

  • None: Deselect both Web and Org Files if you want Perplexity to respond without using external sources.

Why don’t I see Focus in Perplexity’s search bar?

For Enterprise users, Focus has been replaced with Choose sources, allowing you to use Internal Knowledge Search by selecting between Web, Org Files, Web + Org Files, or None.

Who can access the uploaded files?

  • Organization files:

  • All Enterprise users within your organization can download and search the files uploaded by your admin. Go to your settings page and click the “Files” tab to access your organization’s file repository.

  • Files in projects:

  • Only the creator of the project and invited collaborators can access and use the files uploaded to that Project.

  • Learn more about Perplexity projects.

What file types are supported?

Perplexity supports the following file types for Internal Knowledge Search:

  • Excel (XLSX)
  • PowerPoint (PPTX)
  • Word (DOCX)
  • PDF
  • CSV
  • Audio and video

Each file can be up to 40 MB in size.

Note: While PNG and JPEG files are not supported for Internal Knowledge Search, you can still use these image files by attaching them directly to your query. Simply drag and drop them into Perplexity’s search bar.

How are titles and summaries generated for my files?

Titles and summaries for your uploaded files are auto-generated through a language model (LLM). While this helps provide a quick overview of your files, these titles and summaries may contain inaccuracies. If you notice any errors, please refer to the original file content for accurate information.

How can I improve Perplexity’s understanding of my organization?

Your enterprise administrator can provide a brief description of your organization to help Perplexity deliver more accurate and relevant answers.

  • Go to settings
  • Click the Organization tab
  • In the Description field, add relevant information about your organization.

Do I need to enable Pro Search for Internal Knowledge Search?

Enabling Pro Search is not required but highly recommended. Internal Knowledge Search queries often involve more in-depth research across a broader range of sources, and Pro Search’s advanced capabilities provide more detailed and tailored responses.

Practical use cases for Enterprise users

1. Enrich internal analysis with external insights

Conduct analyses on your internal knowledge base and enrich them with external insights from the web.

  • Evaluate customer acquisition strategy

  • Initial question: “Evaluate our customer acquisition approach and provide feedback based on best practices.”

  • Company investment thesis

  • Initial question: “Provide a summary of our current investment thesis on Company Y.”

  • Follow-up prompt: “Have there been recent developments on Company Y that could impact or change our thesis?”

  • Project assessment

  • Prompt: “Based on everything you know about Project X, what are we missing, and what questions should we be asking ourselves?”

  • Follow-up Prompt: “How can we address the potential gaps you’ve identified?”

  • Company investment due diligence

  • Initial prompt: “What are the opportunities and risks of investing in Start-up X, based on the provided materials and latest company news from the web?

  • Follow-up prompt: “Are there any competitors of Start-up X that have recently been funded?”

  • User feedback analysis

  • “What has been the top user feedback on our new feature Y? Consider both internal feedback channels and public web sources.”

  • Sales battle cards

  • “When pitching our product Y to prospects, what common objections are likely to arise? How can we counter these objections? Provide a script.”

2. Generate content based on internal and external insights

Create better content faster by taking input from both materials your team previously created and insights from the web.

  • Sales lead generation

  • “Based on our current sales strategy and the leads we have converted so far, what new prospects would you recommend targeting that could be a good fit?”

  • Sales proposal outline

  • “Create a sales proposal for Company Y that pitches our new product X and makes a compelling case for how it benefits their business. Use insights from past sales proposals and align the pitch with Company Y’s latest strategic priorities.”

  • Presentation preparation

  • Initial prompt: “Help me prepare for our quarterly board meeting by creating an outline of the presentation based on our organization’s major accomplishments from the past quarter.”

  • Follow-up prompt: “Which board questions are likely to come up during the meeting?”

  • Marketing collateral

  • “Create a draft social media post, blog post, and LinkedIn update for the public launch of Product Y, based on the available product documentation.”

  • RFP response

  • “Respond to the attached request for proposal (RFP) from Customer Y, using past project deliverables. Structure the response according to the format outlined in the attached RFP.”

3. Answer specific questions

Use projects as knowledge bases and effortlessly get answers to any question.

  • Portfolio management question

  • Initial prompt: “I’m considering investing in Company X on behalf of my institutional investor client Y. Does this client have a maximum holding rule as part of their portfolio management guidelines?”

  • Follow-up prompt: “Any other client guidelines I should consider for this investment?”

  • Zoning requirements inquiry

  • “Review all documents I’ve uploaded related to NYC commercial zoning requirements. How far do our outdoor tables need to be from the street?”

Best practices for Internal Knowledge Search

  • Keep files updated: Regularly update internal files to ensure the most current information is available.
  • Provide feedback: Provide feedback on search results to improve file rankings and relevance.
  • Collaborate with your team: Encourage team members to use Internal Knowledge Search both in general Perplexity searches and within Perplexity projects for enhanced collaboration and productivity.

Need help?

For additional questions, reach out to our support team, or use the Intercom window to get direct chat assistance from our team and access additional resources.

Perplexity Product Features

How to Delete Scheduled Tasks: Perplexity Tasks vs. Computer Scheduled Tasks

Perplexity Tasks (Scheduled Searches)

Perplexity has two types of scheduled or recurring tasks. They are managed in different places.

Perplexity Tasks are the older recurring tasks, such as daily summaries, scheduled searches, and price alerts. They are not managed through Computer.

Where to find them: Settings → Notifications, or go directly to notifications.

How to delete them:

  • Go to notifications.
  • Click into each task to open it.
  • Select Delete to remove it.
  • There is no bulk delete option. You must delete each task one by one.

Tip: If you are using the desktop or mobile app, open the notifications link in a web browser to make sure the full task management interface loads properly.

Scheduled Tasks in Computer

Computer scheduled tasks are newer recurring tasks created inside Perplexity Computer. They are managed through the Computer section.

Where to find them: Computer → Tasks, or go directly to tasks.

How to stop or delete them:

  • Open Computer → Tasks to see all scheduled tasks.
  • Click any task, then choose Stop task.
  • You can also stop a task from the conversation’s right-hand context pane.
  • Stopping a task removes it permanently.

How to tell which type you have

  • If you created recurring tasks through regular Perplexity prompts, such as “Send me a daily summary of AI news,” those are Perplexity Tasks. Manage them at Settings → Notifications.
  • If you created recurring tasks inside Computer conversations, those are Computer scheduled tasks. Manage them at Computer → Tasks.

Premium Data Sources

Premium Data Sources

We have introduced new Premium data sources, giving all users unprecedented access to deep, authoritative information across business, tech, medical, financial, and market domains—right within the platform.

Which premium data sources are available?

  • Wiley: Unlocks business, STEM, psychology, and medical books, plus wide-ranging journals in health and life sciences, ideal for academic and clinical research.
  • PitchBook Essentials: Delivers detailed firmographics for private and public companies, supporting investment analysis and market mapping.
  • CB Insights: Provides insights into market trends, market maps, and real-time company activity, crucial for startup tracking and innovation monitoring.
  • Statista: Grants access to global statistics, market data, trend analysis, and forecasts—essential for marketing, finance, or industry research.

All premium data is unified—no need for juggling different platforms or separate subscriptions.

You can also combine premium sources freely with Web search or other App Connectors for even richer multi-source answers.

How do I access premium data sources?

Premium sources are easy to use:

  • Hit the + button > Connectors and sources and select the desired premium data source.
  • Or simply type ‘@’ plus the source name in your query (e.g., 0, 1, 2).

You don’t need to log in to these sources or activate them separately; just choose them when starting a new thread from the sources menu, or mention them in your opening message.

Examples:

  • @PitchBook How much capital does The Carlyle Group manage?
  • @Statista What is the market size and growth rate for electric vehicle charging infrastructure globally?
  • @CB Insights What are the main categories of startups working on social determinants of health

Discover more—all in one place

With Perplexity’s premium sources, you can access trusted, in-depth information from renowned publishers and data providers—without leaving the platform.

Every answer is precise, fully cited, and deeply sourced for maximum confidence and clarity.

Using Midpage for U.S. Legal Research on Perplexity

Perplexity provides access to Midpage’s premium legal research database, covering 13.8M U.S. federal and state court opinions, statutes, regulations, and constitutions with an AI-powered citator. Midpage as a premium data source is available to all Perplexity users, and no sign-in or separate subscription is required.

  • Access real, verified primary law – Search 13.8M full-text U.S. court opinions (federal and state), statutes, regulations, and constitutions with hyperlinked citatios you can trust.
  • Get AI-powered citator signals – See negative, caution, and neutral treatment indicators with supporting reasoning and quotations to understand how cases have been cited and treated.
  • Stay current – New cases appear within hours of publication, with regular updates across all federal appellate and district courts plus 50 states + D.C.
  • Built-in for all users – High-quality legal research is convenient and reliable without requiring a separate Midpage account.

Coverage and Data Distribution

Midpage’s database provides comprehensive coverage of U.S. primary legal authority with particularly strong representation in litigation-critical areas:

Key Coverage Areas
  • Federal Case Law – All appellate and district-level courts, including unpublished opinions and select administrative tribunals.
  • State Case Law (50 states + D.C.) – All appellate-level courts, plus unpublished and trial court data from select state jurisdictions.
  • Statutes, Regulations & Constitutions – Full text of codified statutes and regulatory material across federal and state jurisdictions.
  • AI-Powered Citator – Treatment signals (negative, caution, neutral) with supporting reasoning and direct quotations.

How to use Midpage as a premium data source

Simply choose Midpage as a source from the Set sources for search menu when starting a new session. ​

Alternatively, you can mention 0 when starting a new session to select Midpage as a source. ​

You can combine Midpage with Web search or other sources (including other App Connectors) to enrich your answers, integrating legal authority with news, analysis, and internal documents.

What can I ask?

Below you’ll find examples of queries you can ask Perplexity using Midpage’s premium legal data:

  • Finding Cases – What are the four factors courts consider when deciding whether to grant a preliminary injunction under U.S. federal law, and which cases establish them?
  • Jurisdiction-Specific Research – Can a federal court compel arbitration if the arbitration is to occur outside of its district? Focus on 9th Circuit case law.
  • Evidentiary Standards – Find cases in New York state court applying Daubert principles to exclude expert testimony on economic damages in a breach of contract dispute.
  • Administrative Law – What is the standard of review when reviewing agency statutory interpretation, and how has it evolved post-Chevron?
  • Comparative Analysis – Compare how the Second and Ninth Circuits treat qualified immunity in excessive force cases involving pretrial detainees.

How many queries can I run?

You can run a limited number of premium queries each day, with exact limits varying by plan across Free, Pro, Perplexity Max, Enterprise Pro, and Enterprise Max.

Self-Serve Data Deletion

IMPORTANT: Please cancel your subscription before deleting your account to avoid charges after deletion.

You have two ways to delete your account fully: ​

Delete account yourself

You can initiate data deletion directly from your account settings, without the need to contact support or submit a separate request.

  • All account and personal data will be permanently deleted within 30 days. During this period, the account remains disabled and scheduled for erasure.
  • Once deletion completes, it is irreversible.
  • Once deletion completes, signing in with the same email creates a new, empty account, and your previous data will not be restored.
  • If you sign back in before deletion completes, your deletion request is canceled and your account is reactivated.

Steps on how to delete the account:

  1. Sign into the account and visit: details or click on the bottom left corner and then select my account.
  2. Scroll to the bottom of the page to find Learn more.
  3. A pop up will appear and you will be able to confirm the deletion process.

Request account deletion

You can delete your account directly from your account settings, including when exercising your right to erasure under GDPR and similar privacy laws.

  1. Go to Account settings.
  2. Select Delete account.
  3. Review the information and confirm your request.

How can I stop account deletion?

To stop a pending deletion, sign back in as soon as possible. If deletion has not yet completed, your deletion request will be canceled and your account reactivated. Once deletion completes, it cannot be reversed.

Deleting your data

Deleting all your threads

If you want to remove all content you’ve published without deleting your account:

  1. Visit your thread History.
  2. Click the more options icon […] (Three dots) on the top right.
  3. Select “Delete all threads”

This action cannot be undone

Deleting specific Sessions

To remove a single thread or specific post you’ve created:

  1. Visit your thread History.
  2. Click the more options icon […] (Three dots) beside the thread you want to delete or go into the thread in question and click the more options icon and select “Delete” thread
Deleting specific Project

Here’s how to delete projects or Pages, or individual threads

  1. Click on the project or page you wish to delete.
  2. Click on the three (…) next to the title and select Delete.
  3. Please note we will ask you to double check if you wish to delete the content. This action can’t be undone.
Deleting a specific file or image

Files are kept for 30 days by default. To delete uploads, delete the entire session. Learn more about file retention here.

Subscription Plans & Billing

Billing FAQ for Pro Plan Subscribers

How do I upgrade my account via the web app?

  1. Go to your Settings page details
  2. Under Account you will find the Subscription section
  3. Click on Upgrade Plan and select the Plan you wan to use
  4. You will be directed to a Stripe payments page where you can enter your details

What payment methods do you accept?

  • Credit and debit cards (Visa, Master Card and AMEX)
  • Cash App
  • Google and Apple Pay
  • ACH transfer

How do I download a subscription invoice or receipt?

  1. Go to the Perplexity home page.
  2. Click your user profile icon and select Account.
  3. Under Subscription, click Manage Plan.
  4. In Invoice History, find your desired date and click it.
  5. Click Download Invoice to get a copy.

If you purchased your mobile subscription via mobile app - the Google Play Store or App Store app - invoices can be accessed directly through those channels.

How do I update my billing information?

  1. Go to the Perplexity home page.
  2. Click your user profile icon and select Account.
  3. Under Subscription, click Manage Plan.
  4. Scroll to Billing and Shipping Information and click Add payment method.

Invoice that have already been created cannot be changed or updated. Once a user updates their billing information, it will apply to future invoices only.

Trial Promotional Subscriptions - Payment Method Required

If you’ve redeemed a trial or promotional subscription (for example, through a partner promotion), a valid payment method is required to activate and maintain your access. Here’s what you need to know:

Overall Requirements

  • A valid credit or debit card is required for all promotional and trial redemptions.
  • No charges are applied during your promotional/trial period.
  • Unless you cancel before the trial ends, your subscription will auto-renew at the standard rate for the plan you selected during redemption (monthly or annual).

New Users vs. Existing Users

  • New users are prompted to add a payment method during the sign-up and redemption flow.
  • Existing promotional/trial users who receive a payment method prompt must add a credit or debit card within 7 days of the original notification. If no card is added within that window, your account will be downgraded to Perplexity’s free version. Once you add a valid card, your promotional access resumes immediately. However, any missed days will not be added back to your trial period.

Where Notifications Appear

  • Email: You will receive an email notification prompting you to add a payment method. You will also receive a reminder email 14 days before your promotional period expires.
  • In-app: An in-app banner will appear prompting you to add your payment information.

How Do I Update My Payment Information?

Web (desktop):

  1. Go to account.
  2. Scroll to the Subscription section.
  3. Click Manage Plan (or Manage Subscription).
  4. Under Billing and Shipping Information, add or update your payment method.

Do not attempt to update your Perplexity payment information through the Google Play Store or Apple App Store. Doing so will create a new, separate subscription and you will be charged. Always update your payment method via the Perplexity website.

Can I pause my subscription?

No, subscription pausing isn’t available. If you can’t make a payment, you can cancel and upgrade at a later time.

What happens if I don’t renew my subscription?

Your account will revert to the free version after the current billing cycle ends, losing access to Pro features

How do I cancel my plan?

  1. Go to the Perplexity home page.
  2. Click your user profile icon and select Account.
  3. Under Subscription, click Manage Plan.
  4. Click Cancel Subscription.

Additional Refund Scenarios

Forgot to cancel subscription

Refunds may be issued if request follows guidelines mentioned above.

Switching from annual to monthly subscription

Refunds are possible within established timeframe.

Account termination

If your account is terminated for violating Perplexity’s Terms of Use, you won’t receive a refund for remaining credit or prepaid service.

How Credits Work on Perplexity

Credits power Computer, Perplexity’s AI assistant that does multi-step work for you. Your plan may include a monthly amount. You can purchase more as needed.

Quick version: Today, 100 credits equals $1. Light tasks use about 15 to 70 credits. Bigger projects use more. You can see your balance and turn on auto-refill at perplexity.ai/account/usage.

Credit pricing, task ranges, and monthly allowances may change and can vary by promotion, region, or plan. We’ll update this page or your account settings if there are any significant changes to how credits work.

What credits get you

The credits a task uses depend on its complexity. Here’s a typical range by complexity (100 credits = $1). These are examples, not guarantees. Your actual usage may differ.

  • Complexity
  • Typical range (credits)
  • What it gets you
  • Example prompt
  • Light
  • 100–350
  • Research threads, summarization
  • “Summarize an earnings call transcript and highlight risks.”
  • Complex
  • 350–950
  • Multi-step reports, analysis
  • “Build a competitive landscape report with pricing and feature comparisons.”
  • Heavy
  • 875–2,275
  • Complex documents, research-and-code combos
  • “Create a branded pitch deck with market sizing, financials, and competitor analysis.”
  • Mega
  • 2,400–9,800
  • Large projects, video production, data collection
  • “Build an analytics dashboard, collect competitor data, and summarize findings.”

How many credits do you get with your premium plan?

Computer credits are available to Pro and Max subscribers, including Enterprise Pro and Enterprise Max. The number of included credits depends on your plan.

Consumer Pro plans do not start with a set monthly amount. Consumer Max plans start with 10,000 credits a month. Enterprise plans start with a set monthly amount. See your account settings for the exact numbers on your plan.

When you first sign up you might also get a limited-time credit bonus. The bonus is one per account. It does not repeat if you cancel and resubscribe, switch between monthly and yearly billing, or apply a discount or education offer later.

Consumer plans start with:

  • Plan
  • Monthly credits
  • Limited-time bonus
  • Pro
  • No monthly allocation
  • 4,000 credits (one-time, expire in 30 days)
  • Max
  • 10,000 credits per month
  • 35,000 credits (one-time, expire in 30 days)

Enterprise plans start with:

  • Plan
  • Monthly credits
  • Limited-time bonus
  • Enterprise Pro
  • 500 credits per month
  • 8,000 credits (one-time, expire in 30 days)
  • Enterprise Max
  • 15,000 credits per month
  • 45,000 credits (one-time, expire in 30 days)

What credits are used for

Perplexity Ask searches do not consume credits.

Credits are consumed only when using Computer, Perplexity’s AI assistant that does multi-step work for you. Computer understands goals, gathers context, uses the right tools, and carries multi-step work forward across web, files, and connectors.

Computer tasks that are easier will require fewer credits, while those that require significant computing power, such as heavy video generation or large-scale data collection will use more credits.

Checking your balance and usage

Visit perplexity.ai/account/usage to see:

  • Current balances of Bonus, Plan, and Purchased credits
  • Days until your next monthly refresh
  • Usage breakdown by type (text, image, video, audio) and by thread
  • Auto-refill and spending limit settings

For a single task, open the “Usage” dropdown in that thread. You will see how many credits you’ve used and the type of usage it was.

Credits are personal to your account. You cannot transfer or assign to another user. There are limited circumstances for enterprise accounts.

Credit types and resets

There are three types of credits, consumed in this order: Bonus first, then Monthly, then Purchased.

  • Bonus credits are granted at Perplexity’s discretion and expire on the date given when granted. These will be consumed first.
  • Monthly credits (shown as “Plan”) refresh on your billing cycle date. Annual subscribers still refresh monthly on the day you first signed up. Unused monthly credits do not roll over.
  • Purchased credits are consumed last. They stay in your account until you use them. If you do not use Computer for one year, they expire.

Credits are non-refundable. They cannot be redeemed for cash. They cannot be transferred or sold. They only pay for eligible Perplexity features. They carry no value outside Perplexity.

What happens when you run out

  • Active tasks pause. They do not cancel. Your work is saved. Tasks pick up where they stopped once you have credits again.
  • New tasks are blocked — You can’t start new tasks that use credits until you have credits again.
  • Tasks resume automatically — paused tasks pick back up on their own. As soon as you add credits or your monthly credits reset, they keep going.

Want to avoid pauses? Turn on auto-refill. It buys more credits before you hit zero so your tasks keep running.

Purchasing credits

Auto-refill

With auto-refill on, you can buy more credits automatically when your balance gets low. This keeps your tasks from being interrupted. The trigger is 500 credits for Pro and 2,500 for Max. This feature is off until you turn it on. Turn it off anytime at perplexity.ai/account/usage.

To turn it on:

  1. Go to perplexity.ai/account/usage
  2. Toggle on Auto-refill
  3. Then set two things:
  • Refill amount — This is how many credits you buy each time (set any value you want)
  • Monthly auto-refill limit — This is the most you will automatically spend on auto-refill each month.

Auto-refill is off by default. You’ll never be automatically charged for credits without opting in first.

Manual Purchases

You can also choose to purchase one-time credits on your own. If your credits run out and you have not turned auto-refill on, your tasks will pause until you buy more or your monthly credits reset.

Spending limits

Every Max account has a monthly spending cap on total credit charges per billing period. The default is $200, and you can set it anywhere up to $5,000 in your credit settings.

If you hit the cap:

  • Active tasks pause.
  • You will see a notification with the option to raise your limit.
  • No more charges are made until you update settings.
Payment method

If you bought your subscription on the Perplexity website with a card, we default to that same card for credit purchases. If payment on the default card fails, we may attempt to charge other cards on file with Perplexity to avoid service disruption.

You can switch to a different default card for credit purchases at any time. It doesn’t have to match your subscription card.

If you upgraded to Max through the iOS or Android app, you’ll need to add a card on the website to use Computer.

If payment fails, your active tasks pause and we will email you. In most cases, you just need to update your payment method to resume your tasks.

Email notifications

We will email you when:

  • Credit purchase — confirmation when auto-refill adds credits
  • Low balance — when credits are running low (if auto-refill is off)
  • Out of credits — when you run out of credits and tasks pause
  • Spending cap reached — when you’ve hit your monthly spending cap
  • Payment failed — when an auto-refill charge couldn’t be processed

If you change your mind

You can turn off auto-refill anytime at perplexity.ai/account/usage. You can lower your monthly spending cap. You can cancel your plan from account settings. Monthly credits end when your plan ends. Purchased credits stay in your account for one year if unused.

Troubleshooting

What if a task fails?

If a Computer task can’t finish because of an error on our side, we restore the credits used for that task. The restored credits will show up in your balance, usually within a few minutes.

If a task finishes but the result is not what you wanted, credits are not automatically refunded.

Why you might see a negative balance

In a few situations, your balance can briefly go below zero:

  • A task that started before your credits ran out keeps running until it finishes. Any credits used past zero show up as a negative balance until your next refresh or refill.
  • A bonus credit expires while you have a task in progress. The task keeps going against your other credits, which can dip the balance.
  • Auto-refill is catching up after a low balance and the charge has not posted yet.

In each case, the next monthly refresh, auto-refill, or manual purchase can bring your balance back to positive. You are not charged extra for going temporarily negative.

Refunds

Can I get a refund on my credits? Yes, in specific situations. We refund credits when:

  • A Computer task failed because of an error on our side, like a tool crash, a sandbox error, or a network failure. If you think something went wrong, please send us the task URL to support and we will take a look.

  • You see a credit purchase you did not authorize, including an auto-refill charge after you turned auto-refill off.

  • You upgraded to Max by mistake and reached out within 24 hours.

  • Local consumer-protection law requires it. Where the law gives you stronger rights, those rules apply. Specifically, in accordance with applicable law, you may be entitled to a refund of unused credits:

  • In the EU, UK, and Turkey: Within 14 days of purchase; and

  • In Brazil and South Korea: Within 7 days of purchase.

What credits cannot be refunded?

  • Credits used by a Computer task that finished, even if the result was not what you hoped for. The credits paid for the computing power that ran the task.
  • Credits used by a task you stopped while it was still running. The credits already spent up to that point paid for real computing power.
  • Unused credits after you cancel your plan. Monthly credits end when your plan ends. Purchased credits stay in your account for up to one year so you can still use them, but they are not exchangeable for cash.
  • Credits that cost more than you expected for a task that ran as designed. Typical credit ranges are listed under “What credits get you.” If a task surprised you, share the task URL with support and we will incorporate your feedback into improving the product.

How do I request a refund?

Contact support and include the task URL, the charge date, or a screenshot of the charge. The more specific your example, the faster we can look it up. We will write back with our decision and, if approved, restore the credits to your account.

Frequently asked questions

Do monthly credits carry over? No. Unused monthly credits expire at the end of each billing cycle.

Do purchased credits expire? Yes — after one year of inactivity.

What happens to my credits if I cancel? All credits, monthly and purchased, are forfeited when your subscription ends and aren’t refundable, except where required by law.

Can Perplexity revoke my credits? Only in limited cases like fraud, abuse, or Terms of Service violations. We won’t revoke credits just because you use the product heavily within our policies.

Can I use credits on mobile? Yes! You can manually purchase credit packs on iOS, Android, and Samsung Galaxy Store.

I’m a Pro subscriber. How do I get credits? New Pro subscribers may get promotional credits to get started. Bonuses are one per account. Check your credit settings at perplexity.ai/account/usage for any current offers and your balance.

After your bonus runs out, Pro plans do not include monthly credits. To keep using Computer as a Pro subscriber, you buy credits in your credit settings at perplexity.ai/account/usage. You can choose how much to buy, and the credits stay in your account until you use them.

If you want monthly credits included in your plan, upgrade to Max. Max includes 10,000 credits a month.

I was charged for credits I didn’t authorize. Check your credit settings to confirm whether auto-refill is on. If you think there’s an error, contact support at support@perplexity.ai.

How to transfer your subscription

Self-transfer lets eligible Max and Pro customers move their subscription to a different email address themselves, without changing or deleting either account.

Important Notice

Changing your subscription email will create a completely new account. Your original data remains accessible in your original account when signing in with the old email address.

  • All threads, collections, and settings will remain in the old account permanently
  • You’ll need to sign in with the new email address to access the subscription
  • Data cannot be transferred between the two accounts
  • Your original account and all its data remain intact and accessible with your original email

What self-transfer does

  • Moves the subscription from one account to another by updating the email address tied to the subscription.
  • Leaves the original account itself intact (login, data, and settings stay as they are) and simply removes the subscription access from that account.
  • Activates the subscription on the new email/account once the transfer is completed.

Who can use self-transfer

  • Available to consumer Max and Pro customers on standard web-based plans.
  • Not available for Enterprise plans, which are managed directly with your organization’s account team.
  • Only subscriptions purchased on the web (via our website) are eligible; app store subscriptions (Apple/iOS App Store or Android/Google Play) cannot be transferred.

Plans that are not eligible

  • Discounted Education Pro subscriptions are not eligible for self-transfer because the SheerID verification record is permanently linked to the original account.
  • Enterprise subscriptions are ineligible for self-transfer and must be handled through your organization’s administrator or account representative.
  • Subscriptions billed through Apple/iOS or Android/Google Play are ineligible; ownership and billing for these is controlled by the app store provider.
  • Promotion-based subscriptions, including offers through partners such as Airtel or Revolut, are not eligible for self-transfer.

How to transfer your subscription

  1. Sign in to the account that currently holds the subscription you want to transfer.
  2. Go to Account Settings.
  3. Next to your subscription details, click the “Transfer subscription” button.
  4. Follow the on-screen prompts to enter the new email address and confirm the transfer.

Once completed, your subscription will be associated with the new email address, and it will no longer appear on the original account.

Instant Buy + Buy with PayPal

Perplexity users in the U.S. can get 50% back (up to $50) in PayPal Rewards Points on eligible Instant Buy purchases paid with PayPal from November 25-December 3, 2025 and December 11–31, 2025, subject to PayPal’s promo terms and limited quantities. This refreshed offer is only available once per PayPal account and does not apply if a reward was already redeemed during the previous Black Friday–Cyber Monday campaign.

How it works

  • Browse products on Perplexity and look for items marked “Instant Buy.”
  • Select an eligible merchant product and proceed to checkout.
  • At checkout, choose “Pay with PayPal” (not credit card) to qualify.
  • Complete your purchase and receive 5,000 PayPal Rewards Points (redeemable for up to $50) in your PayPal account, typically within 14 days after qualifying.

Eligibility

  • US residents 18+ years old with a valid personal PayPal account in good standing.
  • Purchase must be made via Perplexity’s Instant Buy flow using “Pay with PayPal” at checkout; credit card payments are not eligible.
  • Limit one reward per PayPal account during this offer period; accounts that already redeemed a reward in the November 25–December 2 (Black Friday–Cyber Monday) offer are not eligible for another reward.

Eligible merchants

Products must be marked “Instant Buy” and are available from select merchants, including Wayfair, Abercrombie & Fitch, and thousands of other merchants on BigCommerce, Shopware, and Wix platforms. The reward only applies to the specific item purchased in the qualifying Instant Buy transaction.

Important notes

  • Rewards are administered by PayPal, not Perplexity; for questions about rewards or points, contact PayPal Customer Support.
  • Full PayPal cash back promotion terms are available here: ../assets/img/a83a1717c5_hub/legal/partner-promo-terms-bfcm
  • PayPal may change, suspend, or terminate this offer at any time in accordance with its promotion terms.

Paying in Local Currency

When you subscribe via the Perplexity.ai website (desktop or mobile web), you can choose to pay in your local currency instead of U.S. dollars (USD).

Choosing Your Currency (Web Only)

When creating a new subscription on 0:

  • You can select either your local currency or USD at checkout.
  • The price displayed at checkout reflects your chosen currency, and all future recurring payments for that plan will use that same currency.

This currency selection applies only to subscriptions created directly on Perplexity.ai, not to in‑app purchases through Google Play or the App Store.

​

Does this apply to subscriptions made in the mobile apps? No. Local currency selection currently applies only to subscriptions started directly on Perplexity.ai, not to purchases made through the Google Play Store or Apple App Store.

How are refunds handled for Perplexity.ai website subscriptions? Refunds are always processed in the same currency as your original transaction. If you paid in your local currency, your refund will be issued in that local currency; if you paid in USD, your refund will be in USD. You receive back the exact amount you originally paid in that currency, regardless of any exchange rate changes after your purchase.

Can I switch currencies without canceling my current subscription? No. To change from USD to local currency (or vice versa) for a Perplexity.ai web subscription, you must cancel your current plan and start a new one with your desired currency.

If you subscribed in USD on Perplexity.ai and want to switch to your local currency:

  1. Cancel your existing web subscription.
  2. After cancellation, resubscribe on Perplexity.ai and select your preferred currency at checkout.

Can users in India pay in local currency on Perplexity.ai? Not yet. Users in India currently cannot use Indian rupees (INR) for recurring subscriptions on Perplexity.ai and can only subscribe in USD.

Perplexity Max

What is Perplexity Max?

Max is Perplexity’s most advanced subscription tier built for users who need the highest level of access to the latest, most advanced AI models, Create files and apps queries, and the newest products and features. Available now on all platforms.

Perplexity Max is designed for:

  • Professionals who need the highest-volume access to comprehensive analysis tools.
  • Content creators and writers who require extensive research capabilities.
  • Business strategists conducting competitive intelligence and market research.
  • Academic researchers working on complex, multi-faceted projects.
  • Power-users who want to try out the newest features ahead of everyone else.

Key Features

Max will include everything in the Pro plan plus:

  • The Highest Level Access to Our Full Suite of Advanced AI Models: Quicker access to the latest advanced AI models by OpenAI, Anthropicand others with new frontier models added as they release. Research and Create files and apps also run on more powerful models. With high usage limits. This only applies within Perplexity web and does not apply to programatic access via API, which is billed separately.
  • Max Assistant on Comet:Max Assistant is Comet’s next-level mode, powered by cutting-edge reasoning models that intelligently route every request. Designed for reliability and precision, it delivers exceptional results — especially on complex, challenging tasks. With higher completion rates and smarter outputs, Max Assistant is your go-to for mission-critical work. It may take a little longer, but the results are worth the wait. As a Max subscriber, you receive the highest weekly limit on browser agent queries available across all subscription tiers.
  • Brain (Research Preview):Brain is a self-improving memory system that builds a working model of your projects, people, and files in Perplexity Computer, so Computer can act on requests that build on the work you’ve already done. Learn more about Brain.
  • Extended Access to Create files and apps Queries: Create as many reports or projects as you need using advanced tools to create dashboards, spreadsheets, presentations, and web applications.
  • Priority Support:Max plan subscribers receive the highest level of support, with dedicated infrastructure and faster response times.

Brain on Max (Research Preview)

Brain is rolling out in Research Preview to Max subscribers using Perplexity Computer, free during the preview period. It is rolling out gradually — not every Max subscriber will see it on day one. Learn more about Brain.

What does Brain do for Max users?

Brain runs in the background and builds a working model of the projects you’re driving, the people involved, the documents behind them, and the open loops still unresolved. Computer uses that context to act on requests that depend on what you’ve been working on — with every claim linked back to its source session, file, or connector.

Can I turn Brain off?

Yes. Brain has its own toggle in Computer settings, independent of Search History in Memory. Turning Brain off stops it from writing new knowledge into Computer’s memory from your activity. Incognito sessions are always excluded.

Is my data private?

Yes. Brain is built only from your own activity — your sessions, connected tools, artifacts, and corrections — and is used only to improve your own answers. Every entry links back to the source behind it, so you can open it, check it, and edit or delete anything in Settings → Memory. Models are not trained on what Brain writes into memory.

Billing FAQ

Who can upgrade to Max?

Perplexity Max is currently available only for consumers on a Free or Pro plan.

For Enterprise users, we recommend Enterprise Max, which has been designed for users who need enterprise-grade data security, powerful collaboration tools, and the highest level of access to the latest AI models, Create files and apps and Research search modes, Comet Assistant, and Perplexity’s newest features.

How much does the Max plan cost?

Perplexity Max costs $200/monthly or $2000/annually. Please note, the annual billing option is only available for the web app version.

How do I upgrade to Max (web app upgrade)?

On the web app, you can upgrade by heading to your Settings Page > Account > Subscription > Upgrade Plan

If you subscribed to the Pro plan via the web app, then please use the web app (not Perplexity’s mobile apps) to upgrade to Max. ​ If you upgrade via mobile app, this will create a new separate Max subscription in addition to your ongoing Pro subscription. ​ If you have any questions on this please reach out to Perplexity support: support@perplexity.ai.

If I upgrade from Pro to Max does my balance carry over?

Yes, if you’ve already subscribed via web app we will carry over your balance and bill you the prorated amount for your new subscription. Your upgrade will be effective immediately and you’ll have access to all Max features when you upgrade.

Mobile app upgrades

Android app upgrades: If you have an existing Pro subscription and upgrade to Max via the Android app, Google Play immediately charges the full price of the new subscription. The remaining unused time from the previous subscription is then applied to the new subscription, extending its duration.

iOS app upgrades:If you have an existing Pro subscription and upgrade to Max via the iOS app the remainder of your Pro subscription can be refunded to you. You will have to contact Apple Support with a refund request (Perplexity support does not manage app store subscriptions).

Cancellation and Refunds

EU, UK, or Turkey customers:You are eligible for a refund if the you cancel within 14 days of purchase. This applies to both monthly and annual subscriptions. In the refund request, please specify you are applying for a refund from the EU, UK, or Turkey.

South Korea or Brazil customers: You are eligible for a refund if you cancel your subscription within 7 days of purchase. This applies to both monthly and annual subscriptions. Please specify that you are applying for a refund from South Korea or Brazil in your request.

For all other customers: A refund request window of 24 hours for monthly subscriptions and 48 hours for annual subscription applies. If you cancel within that period, you can request a refund by contacting support@perplexity.ai.

Please note that all other cancellations and downgrades will kick in at the end of your billing cycle. You will continue to have full access to Max features until end of the billing period.

Refunds

What is your refund policy?

Our refund policies vary based on subscription type, regional laws, and account status. Please note we can’t process secondary refund requests after a first one has been granted.

EU, UK, or Turkey customers

You are eligible for a refund if you cancel your subscription within 14 days of purchase. This applies to both monthly and annual subscriptions. Please specify that you are applying for a refund from the EU, UK, or Turkey in your request.

South Korea or Brazil customers

You are eligible for a refund if you cancel your subscription within 7 days of purchase. This applies to both monthly and annual subscriptions. Please specify that you are applying for a refund from South Korea or Brazil in your request.

All other customers:

  • Monthly subscriptions:You can request a refund up to 24 hours after the purchase date. Please include details on why you are requesting a refund.
  • Annual subscriptions:You are eligible for a full refund within 72 hours of the original purchase date and time. No exceptions for requests after this period.

Please note: Sometimes we receive a lot of refund requests and there can be delays in processing them. If you don’t hear back within the specified windows please wait another day or two before contacting us. We always review the date when the refund request was submitted and if it is within the eligible period you will receive your money back. Duplicate tickets may put you in the back of the queue so please avoid sending us multiple requests.

How can I request a refund on the desktop or mobile web?

Visit pro and click “Manage Subscription.” Choose “Cancel Plan.” The option to request a refund will be available along with confirmation of cancellation.

If you do not see this option, contact us through Intercom in your settings or email us.

How can I request a refund on Apple App Store?

Go to 0 and sign in using the Apple ID associated with your App Store purchase. Navigate to your digital purchase history, and tap “Request a refund.”

How can I request a refund on Google Play Store?

To request a refund for an Android subscription, you can email us and please include your google play email address

How can I request a refund on Galaxy Store?

  1. Open the Galaxy Store app → Menu (☰) → My page → Purchase history
  2. Find your Perplexity subscription and tap Refund or Report problem (this option is typically available within ~48 hours of purchase)

If no refund option is available:

  1. Cancel your subscription to stop future charges: Galaxy Store → Menu → My page → Subscriptions → Cancel
  2. Contact Samsung Support at 0 to request a refund for the charge

Additional refund scenarios

Forgot to unsubscribe

Refunds may be issued if the request follows the guidelines mentioned above.

Switching from annual to monthly subscription

Refunds are possible within the established time frame. You will need to start a new monthly subscription and pay the current amount.

Refunds for Enterprise plans

Please note that the same refund policy applies to Enterprise plans. However, if you’ve accidentally purchased an Enterprise Pro or Enterprise Max plan, or need help understanding a charge on your account, please contact us — we’ll be happy to assist.

Account termination

If your account is terminated for violating Perplexity’s Terms of Use, you will not receive a refund for any remaining credit or prepaid service.

Troubleshooting & Support

About the Perplexity Discord Community

Our Discord is where the Perplexity community gathers to discuss Perplexity and broader AI. We have several channels such as prompt libraries, feedback, use cases, and bug reports. Most of our users join because they are interested in:

  • Discussing how to use Perplexity
  • Beta testing features
  • Talking to the devs and team
  • Sharing their use cases and other AI tools

Join the Pro Discord channel via the invite link in your Perplexity account menu. Email support if the link has expired.

How can I contact the Perplexity team on Discord?

You can talk to the Perplexity team on Discord by tagging them. Please do not DM our members and be patient if they do not respond immediately. Our community managers are0and 1. Anyone with the Perplexity icon is a part of the team.

We also have very friendly Community Veterans and Moderatorswho are happy to answer your questions. Please check out the FAQs before asking questions, as we have most relevant information there.

Discord community rules

  1. Keep the content appropriate. No NSFW content, inappropriate usernames, or profile pics. These will result in removal or a ban.
  2. No advertising. No DMs promoting your products, services, or servers. You may post your own project in # 😎 | cool-projects only if it adds value and follows the no-spam rule.
  3. Treat community members with respect. No harassment, hate speech, or malicious behavior. Keep things positive and constructive.
  4. Provide constructive feedback. Make sure your feedback is specific and actionable to help us improve the product and this Discord. Vague negativity isn’t helpful to anyone.
  5. Keep discussions relevant. Stay on-topic and helpful in each channel. Use # 💓 | sharing to post tips, content, or cool discoveries.
  6. Respect privacy. Do not share private or sensitive info about yourself or others. Always get consent before sharing DMs or personal content.
  7. Avoid political and religious discussions. To keep the project welcoming for everyone, avoid political and religious topics and debates.
  8. Don’t repost content without permission. Only share content from others if you have their permission, especially if taken from DMs or other servers.
  9. No spamming. Avoid excessive messages, all-caps shouting, mass pings, or repeating similar posts. Use channels appropriately.

Breaking these rules may result in a warning or ban at moderator discretion. Please also follow Discord Terms and Guidelines.

Cloudflare & VPNs

What is Cloudflare?

Cloudflare is a service that enhances website security and performance. It protects websites from malicious activities like DDoS attacks, spam, and abuse.

Why do I see a Cloudflare loading screen?

Cloudflare performs user checks to limit spam, abuse, and other malicious activities. These checks help maintain the security and integrity of websites protected by Cloudflare.

How do I avoid the Cloudflare screen?

Avoid using VPNs

Using VPNs increases the likelihood of encountering Cloudflare checks. Logging in can help, as Cloudflare screens anonymous users more frequently.

Keep your browser updated

Ensure your browser is up-to-date and configured correctly. Some browsers may not send enough data to verify that you are a legitimate user, leading to more frequent checks.

Clear cache and cookies

If you frequently encounter the Cloudflare screen, try using a different browser or clearing your browser’s cache and cookies.

What should I do if I am stuck on the Cloudflare loading screen?

Refresh the page

Try refreshing the page or accessing the site from a different browser.

Disable VPNs or proxies

Disable any VPNs or proxies you are using, as they can trigger additional checks.

Clear cache and cookies

Clear your browser’s cache and cookies to ensure there are no outdated or corrupted files causing the issue.

Email Assistant Troubleshooting

Please note that Email Assistant will soon be deprecated.

Email Assistant features aren’t working?

If the Email Assistant features aren’t working, try disconnecting and reconnecting through the Assistant settings. To reconnect, click “Disable” at the bottom of the page and go through the onboarding process again. ​

Connecting directly within each email account will not fully reconnect the assistant. Please use “Disable” to ensure a complete disconnection.

See below for more details: ​

Perplexity Assistant is not responding?

To keep your Assistant secure, we verify all emails using authentication checks to confirm that the email is really coming from you. If you are emailing Perplexity Assistant from a custom domain, please ensure that your domain’s authentication records are configured correctly. You can learn more about this here.

Email Assistant doesn’t work with emails that land in different folders in Outlook?

Email Assistant can currently only read and categorize emails in your Inbox, plus some messages in Sent; it does not access custom folders or follow Outlook rules that move emails out of the Inbox. As a workaround, you can pause or adjust rules that bypass the Inbox so emails land there first, then use Label Categories to tag and visually organize messages while keeping them in the Inbox for Email Assistant to process.

Brain

What is Brain?

Brain is a self-improving memory system that builds a working model of your projects, people, and files to give Perplexity Computer the context it needs to move your work forward.

Brain is rolling out in Research Preview to Max and Enterprise Max subscribers using Computer.

How does Brain learn?

Brain runs in the background, reviewing recent activity from sessions, connector updates, artifacts, and corrections, and writing what it learns into Computer’s memory. Each entry links back to its source, so you can check or correct it.

It learns from four things:

  • Your sessions — the tasks you run and the work Computer does with you.
  • Your connected tools — data from connectors you’ve linked, like your docs, messages, or issue tracker.
  • Files and artifacts you create in Computer.
  • Your corrections — so Brain updates what it knows instead of repeating a mistake.

Each run builds on the last: reinforcing what’s still true, updating what’s changed, marking what’s stale. Brain only learns from your own activity — it never pulls in anyone else’s data, and it ignores incognito sessions entirely.

What’s the difference between Brain and Memory?

Memory makes Computer feel familiar. Brain makes Computer more productive.

Memory stores your preferences, interests, and the things you’ve shared, so answers come back personal and relevant.

Brain goes further. It organizes your projects, people, documents, decisions, and open loops into a connected graph — grounded in the sessions, files, and connector data it came from, and kept current as your world changes.

In short: Memory helps Computer know you. Brain gives Computer the working context to move your work forward — and powers a richer Memory over time.

Screenshot: Memory view in Perplexity Computer showing notes, concepts, entities, and workstreams as a connected graph

What does Brain do for me?

Say you’re meeting a customer tomorrow and ask Computer: “What should I present?”

Which customer? What did they ask about last time? Which issues are still open? What’s changed since the last meeting? Which deck is current? Who owns the follow-up?

Brain gives Computer the working context to answer all of that. It starts with the customer, pulls in the latest meeting notes, checks open issues, and looks at the product work those issues block — dropping into Slack threads, call transcripts, and PRs to verify anything uncertain. You don’t get a list of search results. You get a recommendation grounded in your actual work: what to present, what to avoid, what changed, what follow-ups matter.

Brain also lets you resume past work without recapping. Start a new conversation, point Computer at a project from a couple of weeks ago, and it picks up using the decisions, files, and people already in context — instead of making you re-explain everything.

The more you use Computer on a project, the richer Brain’s understanding becomes — so the biggest gains show up on work you return to.

Where do I see what Brain has learned, and how do I edit it?

Open Memory to see everything Brain has learned, organized into Concepts, Entities, and Workstreams — a browsable wiki and graph of your world. Open any entry to read it, see how it connects to related entries, and click through to the original session, file, or source. You can edit or delete any entry, and your corrections feed forward into Brain’s next run.

Screenshot: Brain settings panel in Perplexity Computer showing the Brain toggle, Connected sources toggle, available connectors, and Instructions field

When does Brain run?

Brain runs in the background, not during your active tasks — so it never slows you down. Computer tasks that use Brain actually run faster on average, because Computer reuses what Brain knows instead of rebuilding context from scratch.

Brain itself only maintains your memory. It doesn’t take actions or message you. Over time, Brain is the foundation for more proactive features in Computer — daily digests, skills that improve themselves — but those are controlled separately, and anything that would change how Computer works is surfaced for you to review first.

Can I turn Brain off?

Yes. Brain has its own toggle in Computer settings, independent of Search History in Memory — Settings → Memory settings → Brain. Turning it off stops Brain from writing new knowledge into Computer’s memory from your activity.

For Enterprise organizations, organization admins can disable Brain for organization members in Organization settings → Memory → Enable Brain.

Is Brain private?

Yes. Brain is built only from your own activity — your sessions, connected tools, artifacts, and corrections — and is used only to improve your own answers.

Every entry links back to the session, file, or source behind it, so you can open it, check it, and edit or delete anything in Settings → Memory.

Incognito is excluded. Brain doesn’t read from or write to incognito sessions.

Sensitive-information filtering. Brain uses the same AI-based filtering as Search Memory to reduce the chance that sensitive details — credentials, passwords, and similar — end up in your memory bank. You can always review and clear anything saved.

Model training. AI Data Retention is scoped to searches used to improve models, not to what Brain writes into Computer’s memory. Brain’s own toggle controls its writing. For Perplexity Enterprise users, we never use their data to train our models.

How to Use the Perplexity Android Assistant

With the Perplexity Assistant you can perform a wide range of voice-activated tasks on your Android device, from asking simple questions and taking simple actions, to completing complex tasks end-to-end.

It is a Perplexity layer on top of your device, integrating with many of your apps. This enables you to get assistance without the need to navigate between apps and services.

What is the Perplexity Assistant?

The Perplexity Assistant allows you to perform a wide range of tasks on your Android device, from asking simple questions and taking quick actions to tackling more complex tasks.

It is as a Perplexity-powered layer on top of your device, enabling you to quickly get assistance without needing to switch between apps and services.

How do I start using Perplexity Assistant?

To begin using the Perplexity Assistant, update your Perplexity app to the latest version and follow the onboarding steps by tapping the banner on your home screen. You can also manage the assistant at any time through your device’s assistant settings.

Once it is set as your default assistant, you can activate it by:

  • Swiping from the bottom-left corner to the center of the screen
  • Pressing and holding the power button
  • Pressing and holding the home button

The activation method depends on your device. Here’s a quick video showing you the onboarding steps

Gesture navigation

You can also set-up gesture navigation for the assistant for quicker access. Here’s how:

What are the benefits of the Perplexity Assistant?

Our assistant brings the power of Perplexity directly to your device, allowing you to find answers to any question and take action on the needs you have throughout the day.

The Perplexity Assistant aims to save you time and make your daily tasks easier by supporting with many types of tasks - from small requests to complex actions.

Partner Promotions and Referral Programs

Perplexity regularly runs promotional offers and the discount codes for these offers are distributed through our partners, authorized and official channels, and on our website.

How do I apply a discount code to my account?

Discount codes can only be redeemed on the web, not through the Perplexity mobile apps. To apply a code:

  1. Visit perplexity and either create a new account or sign in to your existing one.
  2. Open the link provided in your email. You will be redirected to Perplexity, and a pop-up will confirm the validity of your discount code. Click “Get Started.”
  3. At checkout, your discount code should be automatically applied. If it isn’t, manually enter the code from the pop-up into the “Add Promotion Code” field on the payment page. Click “Subscribe” to complete your sign-up.

Note: If you have an active subscription through the Apple App Store or Google Play, you’ll need to wait for it to end to apply a discount code.

Why do I need to provide my payment details?

Providing payment details guarantees a seamless switch to a paid subscription after your trial or discount period ends. Rest assured, you won’t be charged at sign-up. Billing will only start once the free period concludes.

You can manage your subscription anytime via your account settings page details under “Manage Subscription.”

Trial Promotional Subscriptions - Payment Method Required

If you’ve redeemed a trial or promotional subscription (for example, through a partner promotion), a valid payment method is required to activate and maintain your access. Here’s what you need to know:

Overall Requirements

  • A valid credit or debit card is required for all promotional and trial redemptions.
  • No charges are applied during your promotional/trial period.
  • Unless you cancel before the trial ends, your subscription will auto-renew at the standard rate for the plan you selected during redemption (monthly or annual).

New Users vs. Existing Users

  • New users are prompted to add a payment method during the sign-up and redemption flow.
  • Existing promotional/trial users who receive a payment method prompt must add a credit or debit card within 7 days of the original notification. If no card is added within that window, your account will be downgraded to Perplexity’s free version. Once you add a valid card, your promotional access resumes immediately. However, any missed days will not be added back to your trial period.

Where Notifications Appear

  • Email: You will receive an email notification prompting you to add a payment method. You will also receive a reminder email 14 days before your promotional period expires.
  • In-app: An in-app banner will appear prompting you to add your payment information.

How Do I Update My Payment Information?

Web (desktop):

  1. Go to account.
  2. Scroll to the Subscription section.
  3. Click Manage Plan (or Manage Subscription).
  4. Under Billing and Shipping Information, add or update your payment method.

Note: Do not attempt to update your Perplexity payment information through the Google Play Store or Apple App Store. Doing so will create a new, separate subscription and you will be charged. Always update your payment method via the Perplexity website.

Can I share this discount code with someone else?

No, discount codes are for individual use only. Sharing them violates our Terms of Service.

Can I apply multiple discount codes to one account at once?

No, only one discount codes can be applied per billing cycle. To add a discount to an existing subscription, go to settings, select “Manage Subscription” in the Pro section, and choose “Update Plan.” Apply your promo code at checkout.

Can I use an expired promo code?

No, expired promo codes cannot be reactivated.

Do you offer any discounts for signing-up?

We do not offer special discounts based on any unique conditions related to geographical location, currency, or demographic status.

How will I be paid?

Dub will issue all pending payments as usual once your referrals complete the 30-day review period. Please ensure your bank account is connected in the Dub dashboard.

Can I submit new referrals?

No, as of November 6, 2025, new referrals are no longer accepted. All prior qualifying referrals will still be evaluated and paid out according to the program’s final payout schedule.

What happens to my referral earnings?

Any qualified referrals completed before the shutdown will be reviewed. Pending payouts will be issued to eligible users after the review process.

Will my past referral activity still appear in Dub?

You can track performance and remaining payouts in the Dub platform until the final commissions are paid out.

Will previously acquired Pro referrals still be honored under my account?

DEPRECATED: How does the”Give a month, Get a month”Referral Program work?

To refer someone, you must be an active Perplexity Pro subscriber.

  • Once subscribed, you’ll receive a unique personal referral link to share with others. Visit details under the Promotions section to get your unique personal referral link to share
  • When someone signs up for Perplexity Pro using your link, they will get one month of Pro for free and you will get one month of Pro in return. There is a cap of 12 successful redemptions per referral link.
  • You can refer as many people as you want, but only one referral reward is applied per billing cycle
  • The reward is applied as a monthly discount to your subscription - it doesn’t stack or accumulate.
  • For example, if you have 13 referrals and 6 have been redeemed, only the remaining 6 months will be applied to your account
  • For users with an active paid or promotional subscription, earned referral months will be applied after the current subscription expires.
  • Subscriptions through Stripe, the Google Play Store, or the Apple App Store are currently eligible to participate.
  • Note: This is the standard Pro Referrals program and is different from the Student Referrals program, which has been discontinued.
Managing your referrals

You can access your referral information and manage referrals directly under the Settings section of your account.

Key Points to Remember:
  • You can refer up to 12 people
  • Only active Pro subscribers can refer others.
  • Only new Pro subscribers can use the referral
If I cancel my subscription, will my referrals remain recorded for future use?

No. If you cancel your subscription, your referrals will be nullified.
